10% spinning reserve factor\nSYSTEM_RESERVE_REQUIREMENT = 0.03  # 3% of total load\nSLACK_BUSSES = [1]  # Reference bus for angle normalization\n\nprint(\"Building optimization model...\")\n\n# Create model\nmodel = ConcreteModel()\n\n# Sets\nmodel.G = Set(initialize=range(len(generators)))\nmodel.B = Set(initialize=range(len(buses)))\nmodel.L = Set(initialize=range(len(branches)))\n\n# Variables\nmodel.Pg = Var(model.G, within=NonNegativeReals)\nmodel.Rg = Var(model.G, within=NonNegativeReals)\nmodel.theta = Var(model.B, within=Reals)\n\n# Generator cost coefficients (linear: c1*P + c0)\nc1 = []\nc0 = []\nfor i, gc in enumerate(gencosts):\n    if len(gc) >= 6:\n        # Extract results\n    dispatch = []\n    total_cost = 0\n    total_gen = 0\n    total_reserve = 0\n    \n    for i in model.G:\n        pg = value(model.Pg[i])\n        rg = value(model.Rg[i])\n        bus_id = int(generators[i][0])\n        pmax = generators[i][8] * baseMVA\n        \n        cost = c1[i] * pg + c0[i]\n        total_cost += cost\n        total_gen += pg\n        total_reserve += rg\n        \n        dispatch.append({\n            \"id\": i + 1,\n            \"bus\": bus_id,\n            \"output_MW\": round(pg, 2),\n            \"reserve_MW\": round(rg, 2),\n            \"pmax_MW\": round(pmax, 2)\n        })\n    \n    # 10% spinning reserve factor\nSYSTEM_RESERVE_REQUIREMENT = 0.03  # 3% of total load\nSLACK_BUSSES = [1]  # Reference bus for angle normalization\n\nprint(\"Building optimization model...\")\n\n# Build bus index map\nbus_index_map = {int(bus[0]): i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n# Filter out zero-impedance branches for power flow calculations\nvalid_branches = [(l, br) for l, br in enumerate(branches) if abs(br[2]) > 1e-10]\nprint(f\"Total branches: {len(branches)}, Valid branches for PF: {len(valid_branches)}\")\n\n# Create model\nmodel = ConcreteModel()\n\n# Sets\nmodel.G = Set(initialize=range(len(generators)))\nmodel.B = Set(initialize=range(len(buses)))\nmodel.L = Set(initialize=range(len(valid_branches)))\n\n# Variables\nmodel.Pg = Var(model.G, within=NonNegativeReals)\nmodel.Rg = Var(model.G, within=NonNegativeReals)\nmodel.theta = Var(model.B, within=Reals)\n\n# Generator cost coefficients (linear: c1*P + c0)\nc1 = []\nc0 = []\nfor i, gc in enumerate(gencosts):\n    if len(gc) >= 6:\n        c1.append(gc[-2])\n        c0.append(gc[-1])\n    else:\n        c1.append(0)\n        c0.append(0)\n\n# Objective: minimize generation cost\nmodel.Obj = Objective(expr=sum(c1[i] * model.Pg[i] + c0[i] for i in model.G))\n\nprint(\"Adding constraints...\")\n\n# Generator limits\nmodel.GenPmin = Constraint(model.G, expr=[generators[i][9] * baseMVA <= model.Pg[i] for i in model.G])\nmodel.GenPmax = Constraint(model.G, expr=[model.Pg[i] <= generators[i][8] * baseMVA for i in model.G])\n\n# Spinning reserve constraints\nmodel.SpinningReserveMaxOutput = Constraint(model.G, expr=[model.Rg[i] <= RESERVE_FACTOR * model.Pg[i] for i in model.G])\nmodel.SpinningReserveCapacity = Constraint(model.G, expr=[model.Rg[i] <= generators[i][8] * baseMVA - model.Pg[i] for i in model.G])\n\n# DC power balance at each bus\ndef power_balance_rule(model, b):\n    bus_id = int(buses[b][0])\n    \n    # Generators at this bus\n    gen_at_bus = sum(model.Pg[i] for i in model.G if generators[i][0] == bus_id)\n    \n    # Branch flows (only for valid branches)\n    branch_injection = 0.0\n    for l_idx, (l_orig, br) in enumerate(valid_branches):\n        fbus = int(br[0])\n        tbus = int(br[1])\n        r = br[2]\n        \n        if fbus == bus_id:\n            tbus_idx = bus_index_map[tbus]\n            branch_injection += (model.theta[b] - model.theta[tbus_idx]) / r\n        elif tbus == bus_id:\n            fbus_idx = bus_index_map[fbus]\n            branch_injection -= (model.theta[b] - model.theta[fbus_idx]) / r\n    \n    # Load at this bus\n    load = buses[b][2] * baseMVA\n    \n    # Power balance\n    return gen_at_bus - load == branch_injection\n\nmodel.PowerBalance = Constraint(model.B, rule=power_balance_rule)\n\n# Transmission line limits (only for valid branches)\ndef transmission_limit_pos_rule(model, l):\n    l_idx, (l_orig, br) = enumerate(valid_branches)[l]\n    fbus_idx = bus_index_map[int(br[0])]\n    tbus_idx = bus_index_map[int(br[1])]\n    \n    flow = (model.theta[fbus_idx] - model.theta[tbus_idx]) / br[2]\n    limit = br[5] * baseMVA\n    \n    return flow <= limit\n\ndef transmission_limit_neg_rule(model, l):\n    l_idx, (l_orig, br) = enumerate(valid_branches)[l]\n    fbus_idx = bus_index_map[int(br[0])]\n    tbus_idx = bus_index_map[int(br[1])]\n    \n    flow = (model.theta[fbus_idx] - model.theta[tbus_idx]) / br[2]\n    limit = br[5] * baseMVA\n    \n    return -limit <= flow\n\nmodel.TransmissionLimitPos = Constraint(model.L, rule=transmission_limit_pos_rule)\nmodel.TransmissionLimitNeg = Constraint(model.L, rule=transmission_limit_neg_rule)\n\n# System spinning reserve requirement\ntotal_load = sum(bus[2] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\nsystem_reserve_req = SYSTEM_RESERVE_REQUIREMENT * total_load\n\nmodel.SystemReserve = Constraint(expr=sum(model.Rg[i] for i in model.G) >= system_reserve_req)\n\n# Reference bus angle\nif SLACK_BUSSES[0] in bus_index_map:\n    ref_idx = bus_index_map[SLACK_BUSSES[0]]\n    model.theta[ref_idx].fix(0)\n\nprint(\"Solving optimization problem...\")\nprint(f\"Variables: {len(list(model.component_variables()))}\")\nprint(f\"Constraints: {len(list(model.component_constraints()))}\")\n\n# Solve\nsolver = SolverFactory('ipopt')\nsolver.options['max_iter'] = 10000\nsolver.options['tol'] = 1e-6\nsolver.options['print_level'] = 2\n\nresults = solver.solve(model, tee=True)\n\nif results.solver.status == 'ok':\n    print(\"\\nOptimization successful!\")\n    \n    # Extract results\n    dispatch = []\n    total_cost = 0\n    total_gen = 0\n    total_reserve = 0\n    \n    for i in model.G:\n        pg = value(model.Pg[i])\n        rg = value(model.Rg[i])\n        bus_id = int(generators[i][0])\n        pmax = generators[i][8] * baseMVA\n        \n        cost = c1[i] * pg + c0[i]\n        total_cost += cost\n        total_gen += pg\n        total_reserve += rg\n        \n        dispatch.append({\n            \"id\": i + 1,\n            \"bus\": bus_id,\n            \"output_MW\": round(pg, 2),\n            \"reserve_MW\": round(rg, 2),\n            \"pmax_MW\": round(pmax, 2)\n        })\n    \n    # Calculate line loadings (for all branches)\n    line_loadings = []\n    for l, br in enumerate(branches):\n        fbus = int(br[0])\n        tbus = int(br[1])\n        \n        if abs(br[2]) > 1e-10:\n            fbus_idx = bus_index_map[fbus]\n            tbus_idx = bus_index_map[tbus]\n            \n            theta_diff = value(model.theta[fbus_idx]) - value(model.theta[tbus_idx])\n            flow = theta_diff / br[2]\n            limit = br[5] * baseMVA\n            loading_pct = abs(flow) / limit * 100 if limit > 0 else 0\n        else:\n            loading_pct = 0\n        \n        line_loadings.append({\n            \"from\": fbus,\n            \"to\": tbus,\n            \"loading_pct\": round(loading_pct, 2)\n        })\n    \n    # Sort by loading and get top 3\n    line_loadings.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    top_lines = line_loadings[:3]\n    \n    # Calculate operating margin\n    total_pmax = sum(gen[8] * baseMVA for gen in generators)\n    operating_margin = total_pmax - total_gen - total_reserve\n    \n    # Create report\n    report = {\n        \"generator_dispatch\": dispatch,\n        \"totals\": {\n            \"cost_dollars_per_hour\": round(total_cost, 2),\n            \"load_MW\": round(total_load, 2),\n            \"generation_MW\": round(total_gen, 2),\n            \"reserve_MW\": round(total_reserve, 2)\n        },\n        \"most_loaded_lines\": top_lines,\n        \"operating_margin_MW\": round(operating_margin, 2)\n    }\n    \n    # Save report\n    with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n    \n    print(\"\\n=== SOLUTION SUMMARY ===\")\n    print(f\"Total Cost: ${total_cost:,.2f}/hr\")\n    print(f\"Total Generation: {total_gen:,.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Load: {total_load:,.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Reserves: {total_reserve:,.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Operating Margin: {operating_margin:,.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"\\nTop 3 Most Loaded Lines:\")\n    for line in top_lines:\n        print(f\"  {line['from']} -> {line['to']}: {line['loading_pct']:.2f}%\")\n    print(f\"\\nReport saved to /root/report.json\")\n    \nelse:\n    print(f\"\\nOptimization failed! Calculate line loadings\n    line_loadings = []\n    for l, (l_orig, br) in enumerate(valid_branches):\n        fbus = int(br[0])\n        tbus = int(br[1])\n        \n        fbus_idx = bus_index_map[fbus]\n        tbus_idx = bus_index_map[tbus]\n        \n        theta_diff = value(model.theta[fbus_idx]) - value(model.theta[tbus_idx])\n        flow = theta_diff / br[2]\n        limit = br[5] * baseMVA\n        loading_pct = abs(flow) / limit * 100 if limit > 0 else 0\n        \n        line_loadings.append({\n            \"from\": fbus,\n            \"to\": tbus,\n            \"loading_pct\": round(loading_pct, 2)\n        })\n    \n    # Solve print(\"Starting optimization...\") start_time = time.time()  result = minimize(     objective,     x0,     method='\"'\"'SLSQP'\"'\"',     bounds=bounds,     constraints=constraints,     options={'\"'\"'maxiter'\"'\"': 1000, '\"'\"'ftol'\"'\"': 1e-6, '\"'\"'disp'\"'\"': True} )  solve_time = time.time() - start_time print(f\"\\nOptimization completed in {solve_time:.2f} seconds\") print(f\"Success: {result.success}\") print(f\"Message: {result.message}\") print(f\"Fun (cost): {result.fun:.2f}\")  if result.success:     # Extract results     dispatch = []     total_cost = result.fun     total_gen = sum(result.x[i] for i in range(n_gen))     total_reserve = sum(result.x[n_gen + i] for i in range(n_gen))          for i, gen in enumerate(gen_data):         dispatch.append({             \"id\": i + 1,             \"bus\": gen['\"'\"'bus'\"'\"'],             \"output_MW\": round(result.x[i], 2),             \"reserve_MW\": round(result.x[n_gen + i], 2),             \"pmax_MW\": round(gen['\"'\"'pmax'\"'\"'], 2)         })          # Sort by loading and get top 3     line_loadings.sort(key=lambda x: x['\"'\"'loading_pct'\"'\"'], reverse=True)     top_lines = line_loadings[:3]          # Calculate operating margin     total_pmax = sum(gen['\"'\"'pmax'\"'\"'] for gen in gen_data)     operating_margin = total_pmax - total_gen - total_reserve          # Create report     report = {         \"generator_dispatch\": dispatch,         \"totals\": {             \"cost_dollars_per_hour\": round(total_cost, 2),             \"load_MW\": round(total_load, 2),             \"generation_MW\": round(total_gen, 2),             \"reserve_MW\": round(total_reserve, 2)         },         \"most_loaded_lines\": top_lines,         \"operating_margin_MW\": round(operating_margin, 2)     }          # Load at this bus         load = bus_data[bus_idx]['\"'\"'load'\"'\"']                  # Branch flows         flow_sum = 0.0         for br in branch_data:             if br['\"'\"'fbus_idx'\"'\"'] == bus_idx:                 flow_sum += (x[2*n_gen + bus_idx] - x[2*n_gen + br['\"'\"'tbus_idx'\"'\"']]) / br['\"'\"'r'\"'\"']             elif br['\"'\"'tbus_idx'\"'\"'] == bus_idx:                 flow_sum -= (x[2*n_gen + bus_idx] - x[2*n_gen + br['\"'\"'fbus_idx'\"'\"']]) / br['\"'\"'r'\"'\"']                  return gen_at_bus - load - flow_sum     constraints.append({'\"'\"'type'\"'\"': '\"'\"'eq'\"'\"', '\"'\"'fun'\"'\"': power_balance_constr})  # Transmission line limits for l, br in enumerate(branch_data):     def line_limit_pos_constr(x, br_idx=l):         br = branch_data[br_idx]         flow = (x[2*n_gen + br['\"'\"'fbus_idx'\"'\"']] - x[2*n_gen + br['\"'\"'tbus_idx'\"'\"']]) / br['\"'\"'r'\"'\"']         return br['\"'\"'limit'\"'\"'] - flow     constraints.append({'\"'\"'type'\"'\"': '\"'\"'ineq'\"'\"', '\"'\"'fun'\"'\"': line_limit_pos_constr})          def line_limit_neg_constr(x, br_idx=l):         br = branch_data[br_idx]         flow = (x[2*n_gen + br['\"'\"'fbus_idx'\"'\"']] - x[2*n_gen + br['\"'\"'tbus_idx'\"'\"']]) / br['\"'\"'r'\"'\"']         return br['\"'\"'limit'\"'\"'] + flow     constraints.append({'\"'\"'type'\"'\"': '\"'\"'ineq'\"'\"', '\"'\"'fun'\"'\"': line_limit_neg_constr})  # System reserve requirement total_load = sum(bus['\"'\"'load'\"'\"'] for bus in bus_data) system_reserve_req = SYSTEM_RESERVE_REQUIREMENT * total_load  def system_reserve_constr(x):     return sum(x[n_gen + i] for i in range(n_gen)) - system_reserve_req constraints.append({'\"'\"'type'\"'\"': '\"'\"'ineq'\"'\"', '\"'\"'fun'\"'\"': system_reserve_constr})  print(f\"Number of constraints: {len(constraints)}\")  # Extract results\n    dispatch = []\n    total_cost = problem.value\n    total_gen = sum(Pg.value)\n    total_reserve = sum(Rg.value)\n    \n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        dispatch.append({\n            \"id\": i + 1,\n            \"bus\": gen_data[i]['bus'],\n            \"output_MW\": round(Pg.value[i], 2),\n            \"reserve_MW\": round(Rg.value[i], 2),\n            \"pmax_MW\": round(gen_data[i]['pmax'], 2)\n        })\n    \n    # Calculate line loadings\n    line_loadings = []\n    for br in branch_data:\n        flow = (theta.value[br['fbus_idx']] - theta.value[br['tbus_idx']]) / br['r']\n        loading_pct = abs(flow) / br['limit'] * 100 if br['limit'] > 0 else 0\n        \n        line_loadings.append({\n            \"from\": br['from'],\n            \"to\": br['to'],\n            \"loading_pct\": round(loading_pct, 2)\n        })\n    \n    # Create report\n    report = {\n        \"generator_dispatch\": dispatch,\n        \"totals\": {\n            \"cost_dollars_per_hour\": round(total_cost, 2),\n            \"load_MW\": round(total_load, 2),\n            \"generation_MW\": round(total_gen, 2),\n            \"reserve_MW\": round(total_reserve, 2)\n        },\n        \"most_loaded_lines\": top_lines,\n        \"operating_margin_MW\": round(operating_margin, 2)\n    }\n    \n    # Generation\nRg = cp.Variable(n_gen, nonneg=True)  # Reserves\ntheta = cp.Variable(n_bus)  # Voltage angles\n\n# Objective: minimize generation cost\ncost = sum(gen_data[i]['c1'] * Pg[i] + gen_data[i]['c0'] for i in range(n_gen))\nobjective = cp.Minimize(cost)\n\n# Constraints\nconstraints = []\n\n# Generator limits\nfor i in range(n_gen):\n    constraints.append(Pg[i] >= gen_data[i]['pmin'])\n    constraints.append(Pg[i] <= gen_data[i]['pmax'])\n\n# Spinning reserve constraints\nfor i in range(n_gen):\n    constraints.append(Rg[i] <= RESERVE_FACTOR * Pg[i])\n    constraints.append(Rg[i] <= gen_data[i]['pmax'] - Pg[i])\n\n# Power balance constraints for each bus\nfor b in range(n_bus):\n    # Generators at this bus\n    gen_at_bus = sum(Pg[i] for i in range(n_gen) if gen_data[i]['bus_idx'] == b)\n    \n    # Load at this bus\n    load = bus_data[b]['load']\n    \n    # Branch flows\n    flow_sum = 0\n    for br in branch_data:\n        if br['fbus_idx'] == b:\n            flow_sum += (theta[b] - theta[br['tbus_idx']]) / br['r']\n        elif br['tbus_idx'] == b:\n            flow_sum -= (theta[b] - theta[br['fbus_idx']]) / br['r']\n    \n    # Power balance\n    constraints.append(gen_at_bus - load == flow_sum)\n\n# Transmission line limits\nfor br in branch_data:\n    flow_pos = (theta[br['fbus_idx']] - theta[br['tbus_idx']]) / br['r']\n    constraints.append(flow_pos <= br['limit'])\n    constraints.append(-flow_pos <= br['limit'])\n\n# System reserve requirement\nconstraints.append(sum(Rg) >= system_reserve_req)\n\n# Fix reference bus angle\nslack_idx = bus_index_map[SLACK_BUSSES[0]]\nconstraints.append(theta[slack_idx] == 0)\n\n# Create problem\nproblem = cp.Problem(objective, constraints)\n\n# Initial guess - scale up current generation to meet load\ntotal_current_gen = sum(gen['pg_current'] for gen in gen_data)\nscale_factor = required_gen / total_current_gen if total_current_gen > 0 else 1.0\n\nprint(f\"Current total gen: {total_current_gen:,.2f} MW\")\nprint(f\"Scaling factor: {scale_factor:.3f}\")\n\n# Solve with warm start\nprint(\"Starting optimization...\")\nstart_time = time.time()\n\n# Try with default settings first\ntry:\n    problem.solve(verbose=True)\n    solve_status = problem.status\nexcept Exception as e:\n    print(f\"Solver error: {e}\")\n    solve_status = \"error\"\n\nsolve_time = time.time() - start_time\nprint(f\"\\nOptimization completed in {solve_time:.2f} seconds\")\nprint(f\"Status: {solve_status}\")\nprint(f\"Optimal cost: {problem.value if solve_status == cp.OPTIMAL else 'N/A'}\")\n\nif solve_status == cp.OPTIMAL:\n    # Extract results\n    dispatch = []\n    total_cost = problem.value\n    total_gen = sum(Pg.value)\n    total_reserve = sum(Rg.value)\n    \n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        dispatch.append({\n            \"id\": i + 1,\n            \"bus\": gen_data[i]['bus'],\n            \"output_MW\": round(Pg.value[i], 2),\n            \"reserve_MW\": round(Rg.value[i], 2),\n            \"pmax_MW\": round(gen_data[i]['pmax'], 2)\n        })\n    \n    # Calculate line loadings\n    line_loadings = []\n    for br in branch_data:\n        flow = (theta.value[br['fbus_idx']] - theta.value[br['tbus_idx']]) / br['r']\n        loading_pct = abs(flow) / br['limit'] * 100 if br['limit'] > 0 else 0\n        \n        line_loadings.append({\n            \"from\": br['from'],\n            \"to\": br['to'],\n            \"loading_pct\": round(loading_pct, 2)\n        })\n    \n    # Sort by loading and get top 3\n    line_loadings.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    top_lines = line_loadings[:3]\n    \n    # Calculate operating margin\n    total_pmax = sum(gen['pmax'] for gen in gen_data)\n    operating_margin = total_pmax - total_gen - total_reserve\n    \n    # Create report\n    report = {\n        \"generator_dispatch\": dispatch,\n        \"totals\": {\n            \"cost_dollars_per_hour\": round(total_cost, 2),\n            \"load_MW\": round(total_load, 2),\n            \"generation_MW\": round(total_gen, 2),\n            \"reserve_MW\": round(total_reserve, 2)\n        },\n        \"most_loaded_lines\": top_lines,\n        \"operating_margin_MW\": round(operating_margin, 2)\n    }\n    \n    # Save report\n    with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n    \n    print(\"\\n=== SOLUTION SUMMARY ===\")\n    print(f\"Total Cost: ${total_cost:,.2f}/hr\")\n    print(f\"Total Generation: {total_gen:,.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Load: {total_load:,.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Reserves: {total_reserve:,.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Operating Margin: {operating_margin:,.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"\\nTop 3 Most Loaded Lines:\")\n    for line in top_lines:\n        print(f\"  {line['from']} -> {line['to']}: {line['loading_pct']:.2f}%\")\n    print(f\"\\nReport saved to /root/report.json\")\nelif solve_status == \"infeasible\":\n    print(\"\\nProblem is infeasible. This could be due to:\")\n    print(\"1. Transmission constraints preventing power flow\")\n    print(\"2. Generator Pmin constraints\")\n    print(\"3. Reserve requirements\")\n    print(\"\\nTrying relaxed version without transmission limits...\")\n    \n    # Try without transmission limits\n    constraints_no_tl = [c for c in constraints]\n    \n    # Remove transmission constraints\n    # Count how many there are\n    tl_start_idx = None\n    for i, c in enumerate(constraints):\n        if hasattr(c, 'args') and len(str(c.args[0])) > 100:\n            # This is likely a transmission constraint\n            pass\n    print(\"Cannot easily remove transmission constraints automatically.\")\n    print(\"Manual analysis needed.\")\nelse:\n    print(f\"\\nOptimization failed with status: {solve_status}\")\n\nEOFPYTHON\npython3 /root/dispatch_solver_cvxpy.py 2>&1 | tee /tmp/solver_output2.log","description":"Run cvxpy solver with better diagnostics","timeout":600000}}],"model":"MiniMax-M2","stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":144,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1098,"cache_read_input_tokens":58981},"service_tier":"standard","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"52450f2a-27af-48c3-be03-562b60dd190b","uuid":"9d94d4b9-6463-4f35-a017-51e2b9bdcb02"}

theta_t) / r\n    flow = (theta[f_idx] - theta[t_idx]) / r\n    \n    # Loading percentage\n    if limit > 0:\n        loading_pct = abs(flow) / limit * 100\n    else:\n        loading_pct = 0.0\n    \n    line_loadings.append({\n        \"from\": fbus,\n        \"to\": tbus,\n        \"loading_pct\": round(loading_pct, 2)\n    })\n\n# Sort by loading and get top 3\nline_loadings.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\ntop_lines = line_loadings[:3]\n\nprint(f\"Top 3 most loaded lines:\")\nfor line in top_lines:\n    print(f\"  {line['from']} -> {line['to']}: {line['loading_pct']:.2f}%\")\n\n# Update report\nreport['most_loaded_lines'] = top_lines\n\n# Save updated report\nwith open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n    json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\nprint(\"\\nReport updated with line loadings!\")\n\nEOFPYTHON\npython3 /root/calculate_line_loadings.py 2>&1","description":"Calculate line loadings from generator dispatch"}}],"model":"MiniMax-M2","stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":771,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":435,"cache_read_input_tokens":67413},"service_tier":"standard","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"52450f2a-27af-48c3-be03-562b60dd190b","uuid":"2781896f-a6d7-4b32-96f2-a8540007776b"}
